## Narration 123

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=fawaz%3Adf57907be352%3Atirmidhi%3A1745)

<p dir="rtl"><strong>The Book on Clothing</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith excerpt</strong> · A quotation preserved from the source; it may contain only part of the narration.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْحَسَنُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ الْخَلاَّلُ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، أَخْبَرَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنْ ثَابِتٍ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَنَعَ خَاتَمًا مِنْ وَرِقٍ فَنَقَشَ فِيهِ مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ تَنْقُشُوا عَلَيْهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1745]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1745.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Jami At Tirmidhi — Arabic</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Jami At Tirmidhi · 1745</strong><br>provider entry identity</p>

<p dir="auto">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API · ara-tirmidhi · entry 1745</p>

<p dir="auto">Numbering: fawaz api hadithnumber</p>

<p dir="auto">Canonical mapping: not inferred</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ أَبُو عِيسَى هَذَا حَدِيثٌ صَحِيحٌ حَسَنٌ ‏.‏ وَمَعْنَى قَوْلِهِ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ تَنْقُشُوا عَلَيْهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ نَهَى أَنْ يَنْقُشَ أَحَدٌ عَلَى خَاتَمِهِ مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1745.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">Source assessment — Al-Albani: Sahih</p>

<p><a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1745.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">Source assessment — Bashar Awad Maarouf: Hasan Sahih</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Anas bin Malik: &quot;The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) had a ring made from silver, so he had &#x27;Muhammad, the Messenger of Allah&#x27; engraved on it. Then he said: &#x27;Do not engrave with it.&#x27; </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1745]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-tirmidhi/1745.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Jami At Tirmidhi — English</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Jami At Tirmidhi · 1745</strong><br>provider entry identity</p>

<p dir="auto">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API · eng-tirmidhi · entry 1745</p>

<p dir="auto">Numbering: fawaz api hadithnumber</p>

<p dir="auto">Canonical mapping: not inferred</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">[Abu &#x27;Eisa said:] This Hadith is Sahih Hasan. As for the meaning of his saying: &quot;Do not engrave with it&quot; - he was prohibiting that anyone have &quot;Muhammad, Messenger of Allah&quot; engraved on his ring</p>

## Narration 124

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=fawaz%3Adf57907be352%3Atirmidhi%3A1746)

<p dir="rtl"><strong>The Book on Clothing</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith excerpt</strong> · A quotation preserved from the source; it may contain only part of the narration.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ مَنْصُورٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ عَامِرٍ، وَالْحَجَّاجُ بْنُ مِنْهَالٍ، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا هَمَّامٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا دَخَلَ الْخَلاَءَ نَزَعَ خَاتَمَهُ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1746]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1746.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Jami At Tirmidhi — Arabic</a></p>

## Narration 125

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=fawaz%3Adf57907be352%3Atirmidhi%3A1747)

<p dir="rtl"><strong>The Book on Clothing</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith excerpt</strong> · A quotation preserved from the source; it may contain only part of the narration.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الأَنْصَارِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي، عَنْ ثُمَامَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، قَالَ كَانَ نَقْشُ خَاتَمِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مُحَمَّدٌ سَطْرٌ وَرَسُولُ سَطْرٌ وَاللَّهِ سَطْرٌ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1747]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1747.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Jami At Tirmidhi — Arabic</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">Numbering: fawaz api hadithnumber</p>

<p dir="auto">Canonical mapping: not inferred</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ أَبُو عِيسَى حَدِيثُ أَنَسٍ حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ صَحِيحٌ غَرِيبٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-tirmidhi/1747.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Anas bin Malik:&quot;The inscription on the ring of the Prophet (ﷺ) was in three lines: &#x27;Muhammad&#x27; on a line, &#x27;Messenger&#x27; on a line, and &#x27;Allah&#x27; on a line</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1747]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-tirmidhi/1747.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Jami At Tirmidhi — English</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">انس بن مالک رضی الله عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی انگوٹھی کا نقش اس طرح تھا «محمد» ایک سطر، «رسول» ایک سطر اور «اللہ» ایک سطر میں، </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1747]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="bn">
<h3>Bengali</h3>

<p dir="auto">আনাস ইবনু মালিক (রাঃ) হতে বর্ণিত আছে, তিনি বলেন, রাসূলুল্লাহ সাল্লাল্লাহু আলাইহি ওয়াসাল্লামের আংটির নকশা ছিল নিম্নরূপঃ এক পংক্তিতে মুহাম্মাদ, এক পংক্তিতে রাসূল&#x27; এবং এক পংক্তিতে ‘আল্লাহ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Jami At Tirmidhi · 1747]</p>
